39

47	> NAT couldn't receive it) — so event triggers can be true push, not just poll-sensors.

116	
117	## 2a. Recurring cadence — incremental loads (the "source keeps producing" clock)
118	
119	> The §2 generator is **one-shot** (backfills N snapshots in a single run). Freshness/
120	> volume/anomaly monitors only mean something if data keeps *arriving* on a cadence.
121	> **Three clocks — do not conflate:** (1) **data-arrival** (source produces new data —
122	> NEW, this section); (2) **load+trigger** (ADF/Airflow picks up the file → triggers
123	> DataQ — §3); (3) **check-run** (DataQ's W5 `schedules` cron, or driven by clock 2).
124	>
125	> **Decisions (2026-06-26) — two layers, don't collapse them:**
126	> - **File creation (clock 1, source-system simulator) = Container Apps Job on a cron schedule.**
127	>   Runs the `mockdata` image (`tick → write files to ADLS landing zone`). Chosen over a
128	>   Function App (pandas/pyarrow is a heavy *batch*, not lightweight event glue — Functions
129	>   add packaging/cold-start friction) and over OS cron; reuses DataQ's Container Apps platform
130	>   + image, cron built in, pay-per-run. **This is *upstream* of the orchestrator** (source drops
131	>   files), which is cleaner/more realistic than the orchestrator also producing.
132	> - **Load + trigger (clock 2) = ADF/Airflow** load to Snowflake/UC → on-success trigger DataQ
133	>   (the path under test). **Deliberately mix two trigger modalities — not all event-driven:**
134	>     - **Event / file-landing** (ADF Storage-Event trigger / Airflow file-sensor or dataset DAG) —
135	>       fires when the Job drops a file. Use for high-frequency/latency-sensitive feeds (e.g. orders).
136	>     - **Schedule / time** (ADF Schedule or tumbling-window trigger / Airflow cron `schedule`) —
137	>       fires on a clock, processes whatever landed in the window. Use for daily/batch feeds
138	>       (e.g. inventory, feedback). **Set the load schedule to fire *after* the file-creation Job**
139	>       (e.g. Job drops inventory 01:00 → scheduled load 01:15) so there's data to pick up.
140	>     - _Why both:_ exercises both ADF/Airflow trigger types **and** DataQ's pipeline-run monitoring +
141	>       10-min polling / gap-recovery fallback (the poll only meaningfully bites on scheduled pipelines).
142	>   Flow C (flat-file in place) has no load step — the Job's file drop *is* the arrival; DataQ reads
143	>   in place (its own W5 schedule or arrival event).
144	>   Proposed split (adjustable): **orders → event**, **tracking → event**, **inventory → schedule**,
145	>   **feedback → schedule** — one event + one schedule per warehouse target (Snowflake & UC) for parity.
146	> - **Required by the Job runtime:** Container Apps Jobs are **ephemeral** → the generator's
147	>   state must move off local disk to **blob** (ADLS). `state.py` needs a pluggable backend
148	>   (local FS today; ADLS blob for the Job). See work items below.
149	
150	- [x] **Generator `tick` mode** — `python -m mockdata tick --feed {orders,tracking,inventory,feedback}` emits **one** batch-suffixed file per dataset (`<name>_b000123.<fmt>`, append-style), not the full backfill. `init` subcommand seeds state + emits the reference loads once. (`mockdata/increment.py` + `state.py`)
151	- [x] **Persisted state** (`state/state.json` + `state/masters/*.parquet`): master pools + climbing id counters (`order_number` never collides) + open-shipment registry (a `tracking_number` from an orders tick gets `ready→in_transit→delivered` on successive tracking ticks). **Verified:** 2 order ticks → ranges 1–40 / 41–80, 0 collisions; tracking# identical across the 3 lifecycle batches; cross-feed FK integrity holds; ruff+black clean
152	- [x] **Per-feed cadence map** (cron) — documented in `mockdata/README.md`: orders `0 0,6,12,18 * * *` · tracking `0 0,12 * * *` · inventory `0 1 * * *` · feedback/reference `0 1 */2 * *`; masters via `init` (rare). Anomaly flags `--volume-drop`/`--stale`/`--schema-drift` + `--issues` per tick
153	- [x] **`state.py` pluggable backend** — `StateBackend` seam: `LocalStateBackend` (default) + `AdlsStateBackend` (Gen2 blob via `DefaultAzureCredential`, azure libs lazy-imported). `make_backend()` picks by URI (`adls://<acct>/<fs>/<prefix>` else local path). **Verified:** local tick flow intact post-refactor; ruff+black clean. ADLS impl coded, runnable against real ADLS post-apply
154	- [x] **`Dockerfile`** (`python:3.13-slim` + pandas/pyarrow/azure; `ENTRYPOINT python -m mockdata`) + `.dockerignore` (keeps secrets.sh/terraform/out/state out of context). **Verified:** image builds; in-container `init`+`tick` write the route layout; **state survives across separate container runs** (mounted vol here; ADLS backend in the Job); `azure SDK OK` in image
155	- [x] **emit output backend → ADLS** — factored the byte-storage primitive into a shared `storage.py` `Store` seam (`LocalStore` + `AdlsStore` + `make_store`, used by **both** `state.py` and `emit.py` — one ADLS-auth impl, no dup). `emit.py` routes every write (csv/parquet/json via buffers + jsonl `append_text`) through the store; `--out adls://<acct>/<fs>[/<prefix>]` now writes the landing zone directly. **Verified:** local backfill+tick intact; ruff+black clean; in the image, `make_store` routing + lazy azure import + DataLake client construct, and a write reaches the Azure auth layer (fails only on missing identity — managed identity supplies it in the Job). **Job is now fully standalone** (no mount needed); only live ADLS write awaits apply
156	- [x] **Container Apps Job (cron)** per feed — **APPLIED + SEEDED**: 4 jobs (`mockdata-orders`/`-tracking`/`-inventory`/`-feedback`) provisioned on their crons, pulling `dataqacr3erlgd.azurecr.io/mockdata:latest`. **`init` run once (2026-06-27)** against `adls://dataqharness3erlgd/landing` (state at `…/landing/state`, tier S seed 42) — seeded masters + reference loads + `state.json`; a verification `tick --feed orders` emitted batch `b000001` (304 rows) cleanly, so the scheduled ticks (same command) will run. _Re-run `init` after a `terraform destroy`/recreate (state lives in the storage account)._
157	- [x] **ADF/Airflow load — APPLIED + RUNNING LIVE**: Airflow CeleryExecutor (schedule DAGs `flow_a_snowflake_load` 9 feeds / `flow_a_uc_reference` 8 dims / `flow_b_medallion`; event DAG `flow_a_payments_event`), ADF `pl_flow_a_orders` (blob-event) + `pl_flow_a_customers` (timer). **No trigger push** — decoupled (DataQ polls; see §3a). The only remaining gate is DataQ's *own* deploy so its poller can read the ADF/Airflow REST APIs (separate deploy track). NB: the ADF blob-event trigger needed the ADF MI granted on the storage account to *fire* — a role assignment the Contributor SP can't make (manual grant)

227	## 6. Performance / scale testing (post-v1 / backlog)
228	
229	> Rides §2's parameterizable volume (`--seed` + a `--volume` knob): generate **S / M / L / XL** of the same
230	> datasets, run, measure, record. **Baseline-first** — establish numbers + a regression budget; no hard SLAs
231	> yet (set them once we know where the knees are). Standalone harness script; the **QA/QE agent (§4)** can
232	> invoke it later as a perf-smoke. Needs §2 (generators) + §3 (the run paths) green first.
233	
234	**Lead axes (do these first):**
235	- [ ] **Run scaling** *(headline)* — GX run wall-clock vs **rows × #checks-per-suite**, per datasource (Snowflake · UC · flat-file CSV+Parquet). Capture throughput (rows/sec) + **worker memory peak** (GX pulls the batch in-memory) — and note whether time is in the warehouse query or GX itself.
236	- [ ] **Profiler latency** — profile a **wide** table (many columns) to quantify the known **N+1 per-column top-values** cost ([#327](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/327)) and the payoff once it's batched.
237	- [ ] **DB growth** — p95 latency of `/results`, `/dashboard/summary`, `/runs` as `results`/`runs` grow to 100k+ rows; validates the retention-sweep index ([#323](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/323)).
238	
239	**Secondary axes:**
240	- [ ] **Concurrency / throughput** — N suites at once: Celery pool saturation, the 60s scheduler beat under many due schedules (`FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`), run-now storms → queue wait time.
241	- [ ] **Flat-file batch resolution at scale** — resolving the latest batch among a large landing zone (thousands of files) — the list+regex cost.
242	
243	**Method:**
244	- [ ] One script: param = datasource × volume tier × #checks; emits a CSV/JSON of (metric, value) per run
245	- [ ] Record a **baseline table** (commit it with the QA/QE agent in §4) + a **regression budget** (e.g. fail if a tier regresses > X% vs baseline) — so perf regressions are caught, not just measured once
246	- [ ] Capture both **app** metrics (run duration, profiler latency, API p95) and **resource** metrics (worker mem peak, DB CPU)
247	- [ ] Feed findings back: confirm/close #327 (profiler) + #323 (retention index); file new issues for any knee found

161:- [x] ✅ Check types for flat files: schema validation, row count, null checks, ~~freshness by filename date~~ — `FlatFileCheckRunner` (`datasources/flatfile.py`): downloads the file (S3/ADLS) into a pandas DataFrame and runs the suite's GX expectations against GX's **pandas DataFrame asset**, so all GX expectation-kind checks (schema/row-count/null/value checks) run against flat files. Extracted the shared GX machinery — snake_case→GX-class translation, GX-result→`SuiteOutcome` mapping, and the suite/validation-definition `run_expectations` flow — into `datasources/gx_runner.py` (reused by the Snowflake runner and the future UC runner; no duplication). Flat-file IO (`download_bytes` + full-file `read_dataframe`) moved to `flatfile.py` behind primitives (raw config + resolved secret, not the ORM — matches `build_snowflake_runner`), and the column profiler now shares it. Because GX runs **in-process on the DataFrame**, the run path is fully unit-tested with a canned frame (real GX execution, not mocked) — only the network download is the deferred-smoke seam. 16 new tests; `gx_runner` 100%, `flatfile` 76% (only `download_bytes` uncovered). _Connection-type → runner dispatch (Snowflake vs flat-file) is the Week-5 `run_suite` wiring; **freshness-by-filename** is the reserved `freshness` monitor kind (deferred, ADR 0012), not a GX expectation._

304

306	
307	### DevOps & deployment (6 tasks — 1/6, 1 partial early)
308	- [ ] 🟡 Containerise FastAPI + React + Celery + Redis — backend `Dockerfile` + `api`/`worker` compose services landed early ([PR 4a](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/74)); React image + ACR/ACA still pending
309	- [ ] ⬜ Push images to Azure Container Registry
310	- [ ] ⬜ Deploy to Azure Container Apps (API + Celery worker) + Azure Static Web App (React UI) — wire CORS middleware for Static-Web-App → Container-Apps cross-origin ([PR #40 nit](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/40)); override hardcoded `dataq:dataq` Postgres creds + all secrets via Container Apps secret refs ([PR #39 nit](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/39))
311	- [x] ✅ CI/CD pipeline — lint, test, build, deploy. The `Deploy` workflow (`workflow_dispatch`) is **validated end-to-end** (build → push GHCR → `alembic upgrade head` migrate job → ACA API/worker roll → SWA deploy, all green on `v6`). The first real run exposed two `deploy.yml` bugs — migrate job ran uvicorn not alembic ([#401](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/401)), frontend `pnpm/action-setup` version unset ([#402](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/402)) — fixed in [#403](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/403). **GHCR package→repo connect done** (Actions-access grant; CI's `GITHUB_TOKEN` now pushes — ADR [0023](adr/0023-container-image-registry-ghcr.md)). _(Push-trigger-on-merge stays off for now — manual dispatch only.)_
312	- [x] ✅ **Post-deploy bug: Celery beat crash on startup (closes [#405](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/405))** — the embedded `worker -B` beat process was re-nulling `self.lock` inside `AzureLogHandler.createLock()` on Python 3.13 (a recurrence of the #393 lock issue in the beat fork), silently killing **all** periodic tasks — orchestration polling (ADF + Airflow), scheduled-suite dispatch, gap recovery, and the sample-failure purge. Fix: override `createLock` in `backend/app/core/logging.py` to be idempotent (only ever create a missing lock, never null/swap one) + a network-free regression test in `backend/tests/core/test_logging.py`. — **[#407](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/407)**
313	- [x] ✅ **Post-deploy bug: deployed app couldn't read Key Vault (closes [#406](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/406))** — `AzureKeyVaultStore` called `DefaultAzureCredential()` with no args, but the api+worker containers run a USER-assigned managed identity and `AZURE_CLIENT_ID` was unset in the Container Apps env, so every `get_secret` call failed (`SecretNotFoundError` / `DefaultAzureCredential failed`). This blocked connection tests, suite runs, AND orchestration polling. Fix: add `AZURE_CLIENT_ID = azurerm_user_assigned_identity.app.client_id` to `local.app_env` in `deploy/terraform/containerapps.tf` (shared by the api and worker). — **[#408](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/408)**
314	- [ ] 🟡 **Redeploy to image `:v7` with #405+#406 fixes** — backend image `:v7` built+pushed from main, api+worker rolled to v7, App Insights re-enabled on the worker (the #405 mitigation of temporarily dropping `APPLICATIONINSIGHTS_CONNECTION_STRING` on the worker is reverted). Verified live: beat starts clean (zero NoneType crashes), api healthy (401, auth-enforced), orchestration polling executes and reads KV secrets successfully. `image_tag` default bumped v4→v7 in `deploy/terraform/variables.tf`. — **[#409](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/409)** _(open)_
315	- [ ] ⬜ Application Insights integration — traces, errors, slow queries, Celery task metrics _(keep the export behind the structlog handler seam in `core/logging.py`; if a vendor-neutral path is wanted, route via OpenTelemetry/OTLP so the backend is swappable — per [ADR 0010](adr/0010-provider-agnostic-infrastructure-seams.md). App Insights stays the only v1 backend; do not abstract speculatively)_
316	- [ ] ⬜ Real-vault integration test for `AzureKeyVaultStore` lazy-import branch (currently 0% coverage) ([PR #56 nit](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/56))

347	### Consistency & transaction hardening (added — from the ACID/consistency review) (4 items — 1/4)
348	> Follow-ups surfaced by the ACID / SCD review (2026-06-20). Single-DB ACID is sound (Postgres + unit-of-work + idempotent `ON CONFLICT` upserts); these close the deliberate gaps. Not in the original 100-task roadmap — tracked here so they aren't lost.
349	- [ ] ⬜ **Trigger-dedup race** — `_trigger_suites` is a non-atomic SELECT-then-INSERT with no unique constraint on `runs(suite_id, triggered_by)`; concurrent ingestion (webhook / poll / gap-recovery) can double-trigger a suite run. Fix = partial unique index + `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`. [#308](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/308)
350	- [ ] ⬜ **Stuck-run reaper** — a run committed `queued` before `send_task` is orphaned forever if the process dies in that window (no reaper; gap-recovery covers `pipeline_runs`, not `runs`). Beat janitor to fail/re-dispatch runs stuck past a threshold. [#309](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/309)
351	- [x] ✅ **History/audit strategy (ADR)** — decided in [ADR 0020](adr/0020-history-and-audit-strategy.md): per-entity **Type-4** snapshot tables where config history is needed (no SCD-2; credentials never snapshotted; cascade-delete accepted; soft-delete + cross-entity audit log deferred). **First implementation: `connection_versions`** (connection name/config history, snapshot on create + name/config update; secret-only rotation records nothing). [#310](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/310) _(audit-log + soft-delete remain deferred there)_
352	- [ ] ⬜ **Minor robustness (no separate issue)** — concurrent check-edit collision → 409 not an unhandled 500 (`IntegrityError` on the `uq_check_versions_check_version` backstop); explicit `rollback` in `get_db`'s `except`; document the "read-modify-write needs a unique constraint or `FOR UPDATE`" convention in CONTRIBUTING (no row-locking exists today, so every check-then-write is only as safe as its constraint).
353

Deliberately unscoped (bypasses owned-or-shared). `/me` exposes `is_workspace_admin`.

* feat(checks): author freshness/volume monitor checks (ADR 0012) [backend]

Opens the create/update/import path for the freshness & volume monitor kinds
(#426 landed the run engine; this lets users actually author them).

- check_service: widen _V1_SUPPORTED_KINDS to expectation + MONITOR_KINDS;
  validate_monitor_check gates monitors to SQL datasources (Snowflake / Unity
  Catalog, like custom-SQL), validates config shape via the shared
  monitors.validate_monitor_config, and REQUIRES a fail/critical age threshold
  for freshness — the silent-green guard flagged in the #426 review (freshness
  has no in-config bound, so without a threshold it can never fail).
- monitors.validate_monitor_config: static (DB-free) column/bounds validation,
  reused by the author path and implicitly by the run path.
- suite_io_service: apply the same monitor validation at suite-import time.
- Reserved kinds (schema_drift/anomaly/comparison) still 422.

Tests: 9 new check-API cases (freshness/volume create+update, threshold guard,
missing column, inverted range, flat-file rejection, still-reserved kind);
full backend suite 1031 passed.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Entire-Checkpoint: 5991e7e8020c

* feat(checks): freshness/volume monitor authoring UI (ADR 0012) [frontend]

The check editor can now author the freshness & volume monitor kinds, not just
GX expectations — completing the #426 monitor work end to end.

- expectationCatalog: model a check `kind` + monitor threshold semantics;
  add Freshness (timestamp column) + Volume (min/max rows) catalog entries;
  SQL-datasource-gate the monitor categories (Snowflake / Unity Catalog), like
  Custom SQL. Schema-drift stays the only reserved category.
- checkForm: send `kind` in the create/update payload (defaults expectation).
- SeverityThresholdFields: kind-aware help + bounds (freshness = age-hours,
  unbounded; volume = deviation-%, 0-100), and REQUIRE a fail/critical threshold
  for freshness (the #426 silent-green guard, mirrored from the backend 422).
- CheckNew/CheckEdit: render monitor config + thresholds; hide the GX-only
  dry-run for monitors; lock a monitor's type in edit (kind is immutable).

Tests: monitor catalog gating + spec model (expectationCatalog); a new
CheckNewMonitor page-flow suite (freshness/volume authoring + the required-
threshold guard); existing payload assertions updated for `kind`. Full
frontend suite 290 passed; build clean.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Entire-Checkpoint: d27f9378ef6a

* fixup! feat(checks): author freshness/volume monitor checks (ADR 0012) [backend]

Code-review response (3-angle review of #437):
- validate_monitor_check now asserts expectation_type == monitor:<kind> (the new
  monitors.monitor_expectation_type helper) — a mismatched/junk type would
  mislabel result rows + could smuggle a custom-SQL type past its guard.
- Freshness now requires a POSITIVE fail/critical threshold (0 was the inverse
  silent-green footgun: fail>=0h always fails); use the monitors.FRESHNESS
  constant, not a literal.
- Frontend: volume severity-threshold inputs drop the max:100 cap — a spike's
  deviation-% is unbounded (10x = 900%), so the cap made spike monitoring
  unconfigurable.
- Frontend: edit PATCH omits the immutable 'kind' (don't rely on Pydantic
  extra='ignore').

Tests: +type/kind-mismatch + zero-threshold rejection (backend); volume-max +
edit-payload assertions updated. Backend 1033 passed; frontend 290 passed.

10b576b0fd0f120e02e33858192b33f7f5f10ba6
docs: compliance posture (GDPR/CCPA/HIPAA) — technical controls + gap list (#436)
Maps DataQ's technical data-handling controls to the major data-protection
regimes and records the honest gap list, so a customer security review has an
answer and the path to a v2.x 'processor-grade controls' claim is explicit.

- Frames the BYOL controller/processor split (ADR 0013): the deploying org is
  the controller; DataQ is the processor providing the technical controls.
- v1 strengths: logger PII redaction, default-redact samples, retention purge,
  SecretStore, TLS, suite-scoped authz, config-change history.
- v2.x gaps filed in Backlog: access audit trail (#431, the HIPAA gate),
  data-subject rights (#432), authoritative PII/PHI classification (#433),
  region/residency assertion (#434), assert-at-rest + CMK (#435).
- Explicit non-goal: no legal 'certified/compliant' claim — org-side artifacts
  (DPA/BAA/DPIA/breach runbook) stay the controller's.

Adds the progress.md 'Deferred to post-v1' pointer row.

64	| **Compliance posture (GDPR / CCPA-CPRA / HIPAA)** — technical-controls audit + gap list. v1 = privacy-by-design (PII redaction, retention purge, least-priv secrets, suite-scoped authz, BYOL controller/processor split). v2.x gaps to claim "processor-grade controls": access audit trail (#431, HIPAA gate), data-subject rights (#432), authoritative PII/PHI classification (#433), residency assertion (#434), CMK (#435). Not a legal certification — org-side artifacts (DPA/BAA/DPIA) stay the controller's. | [docs/compliance-posture.md](compliance-posture.md) + #431–#435 (Backlog) | 🔵 |
65	| **Expectation expansion, marketplace & LLM-assisted authoring** — the "intelligence" layer on the DQ loop: (1) the 5 high-ROI GX built-ins (`mostly` + compound/cross-column + type/set/date; aggregates likely a 3rd monitor kind, not a GX expectation), (2) LLM custom-SQL generator, (3) LLM curated check-suggestions, (4a) curated server-served expectation catalog (adds the server-side allowlist v1 lacks). LLM integration = an admin-configured, default-off, **BYO-credential `LLMProvider` seam** (Anthropic default · Azure Foundry · Bedrock · any OpenAI-compatible endpoint incl. local Ollama/vLLM/TGI), customer pays, no key-proxy, schema-only (PII-redacted) context. | [docs/post-v1-dq-intelligence-notes.md](post-v1-dq-intelligence-notes.md) | 🔵 |

=== admin-ui head ===
# Post-v1 notes — Admin, access model & UI/IA (deferred design)

> **Status: deferred to post-v1.** Captured so the design intent isn't lost. We
> intentionally did **not** build the admin/IAM console in v1. For a single-tenant
> tool used by one trusted data team, a full RBAC console (admin write console,
> user-disable, per-connection ownership restrictions, an access matrix) is
> gold-plating. The market-leading DQ tools (Great Expectations, Soda, dbt tests,
> Monte Carlo) lead with **checks → results → trends → alerts**, not user-lifecycle
> management. v1 ships suite-level sharing + a read-only admin view; everything
> else is recorded here for later.
>
> Related issues: **#411** (admin workspace-wide view) and **#412** (admin write
> actions), both milestoned _Backlog (post-v1 / testing)_.

## v1 access model (what actually ships)
=== dq-intel head ===
# Post-v1 notes — Expectation expansion, marketplace & LLM-assisted authoring (deferred design)

> **Status: deferred to post-v1.** Captured so the design intent isn't lost. None of
> this is a v1 blocker — the v1 DQ loop (checks → results → trends → freshness/volume
> monitors → alerts → MCP) stands on its own. These are the "intelligence" layer on
> top of it.
>
> **The big enabler (already true in v1):** there is **no server-side expectation
> allowlist** — [`gx_runner`](datasources/gx_runner.py) title-cases *any*
> `expectation_type` string into a GX class, and `checks.config` is free-form JSONB.
> So "add an expectation" / "wire in a check" is mostly a **frontend-catalog + config-
> validation** problem, not a backend-engine one. That's why the items below are
> cheaper than they look.

## The four themes
=== compliance head ===
# Compliance posture — GDPR / CCPA-CPRA / HIPAA (technical controls & gaps)

> **What this is:** a map of DataQ's **technical** data-handling controls against the
> **technical** requirements of the major data-protection regimes, plus an honest gap
> list. **What this is not:** a legal compliance certification. Much of GDPR/HIPAA is
> *organizational* (DPAs, BAAs, DPIAs, consent, lawful basis, breach process) and is
> the deploying organization's responsibility, not the codebase's. Treat the "v2.x
> target" column as **engineering work that would let us credibly claim alignment** —
> the legal claim still needs counsel/DPO sign-off.

## 0. The single most important framing: roles & deployment model

DataQ ships as **customer-deployed BYOL** (ADR 0013), not multi-tenant hosted SaaS.
That fixes the data-protection role split:

## How to update this file

When merging a PR:

1. Find the task(s) it implements in the relevant week.
2. Flip `⬜` → `✅` (or `⬜` → `🟡` if partial).
3. Append the PR link: `— [PR #N](https://github.com/.../pull/N)`.
4. Update the per-week subtotal at the bottom of the week.
5. Update the **Snapshot** table at the top (task count, open PRs/issues) and the **Aggregate** table.
6. If the PR added an out-of-roadmap task (e.g. ADR-driven scope change), add a row with the note.

PR-template checkbox enforces this. If the change is purely tooling / docs that doesn't map to a roadmap task, tick the "N/A" checkbox.

Four themes: (1) the 5 high-ROI GX built-ins,\n(2) LLM custom-SQL generator, (3) LLM curated check-suggestions, (4a) curated server-served\ncatalog + allowlist. LLM integration = admin-configured, default-off, BYO-credential `LLMProvider`\nseam (schema-only, PII-redacted context).\n\n| # | Title |\n|---|---|\n| [#124](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/124) | Add DQ-dimension classification to checks (Completeness / Uniqueness / Validity / …) |\n| [#286](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/286) | Apache Iceberg v2 / v3 table-format support |\n\n*(The built-ins / LLM / marketplace work is design-captured but not yet filed as discrete issues —\nfile from the detail doc when picked up.)*\n\n---\n\n## Theme 3 — Admin, access model & UI/IA\n\nFull design: **[post-v1-admin-ui-notes.md](post-v1-admin-ui-notes.md)**. v1 ships suite-level sharing\n+ a read-only workspace-admin view (#289). A full RBAC console is gold-plating for a single-tenant\ntrusted-team tool; the market leaders lead with checks→results→trends→alerts, not user-lifecycle.\n\n| # | Title |\n|---|---|\n| [#411](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/411) | Workspace-admin: workspace-wide view on Dashboard + Results (both are owned-or-shared scoped today) |\n| [#412](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/412) | Admin page is read-only — allow workspace-admin write actions (manage shares / suites) |\n\n---\n\n## Theme 4 — Compliance (GDPR / CCPA-CPRA / HIPAA)\n\nFull design: **[compliance-posture.md](compliance-posture.md)**. v1 is privacy-by-design (logger PII\nredaction, default-redact samples, retention purge, SecretStore, suite-scoped authz, BYOL\ncontroller/processor split). These close the gaps for a credible v2.x \"processor-grade controls\" claim.\n\n| # | Gap | Regime hook |\n|---|---|---|\n| [#431](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/431) | 🔴 **G1** data-access audit trail (who read which result/sample) | HIPAA §164.312(b) — the HIPAA gate |\n| [#432](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/432) | 🟠 **G2** data-subject-rights machinery (erasure / access / portability) | GDPR Art 15/17/20, CCPA |\n| [#433](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/433) | 🟠 **G3** authoritative PII/PHI classification (warehouse tags over name heuristic) | — |\n| [#434](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/434) | 🟠 **G4** region/residency assertion + enforcement | GDPR Ch. V; LLM transfer vector |\n| [#435](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/435) | 🟡 **G5** assert encryption-at-rest in IaC + offer CMK | — |\n\n---\n\n## Theme 5 — Alerting depth (beyond the v1 Teams/Slack/email seam)\n\nv1 ships the `ResultPublisher` seam with Teams + Slack + email, severity routing, dedup, snooze.\nThese enrich and de-risk it:\n\n| # | Title |\n|---|---|\n| [#416](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/416) | Enrich Slack/email alerts: deep link to run, per-check expected-vs-observed, actionable sample, run metadata |\n| [#415](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/415) | Actionable failing-row samples: column-aware redaction (PII vs identifier vs safe) — *partly done in #417; classification remainder here* |\n| [#386](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/386) | Tie `dedup._RANK` to a shared severity source so it can't drift from `routing.route_for` |\n| [#387](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/387) | `suppression.py` should early-return `False` on `run.status == 'failed'` |\n| [#388](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/388) | Single-source the `alert_on` literals (model CHECK ↔ validation) to prevent drift |\n| [#389](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/389) | Rename `teams_webhook_secret_name` → channel-neutral before a 2nd ResultPublisher ships |\n\n---\n\n## Theme 6 — Consistency & transaction hardening\n\nSurfaced by the ACID/SCD review (single-DB ACID is sound; these close the deliberate gaps):\n\n| # | Title |\n|---|---|\n| [#308](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/308) | 🟠 Double-trigger race in `_trigger_suites` (non-atomic dedup, no unique constraint) |\n| [#310](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/310) | History/audit strategy remainder (ADR 0020 landed `connection_versions`; audit-log + soft-delete deferred) |\n| [#371](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/371) | Validation-error handler chokes on Pydantic `field_validator` `ValueError` ctx |\n| [#372](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/372) | `SecretStore` has no `delete`: webhook/connection secrets orphan on clear/delete |\n| [#306](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/306) | Validate provider/env query params on orchestration read endpoints (silent `200 []` on typo) |\n\n---\n\n## Theme 7 — Performance & scale\n\nRides the harness's parameterizable volume (ADR 0021; HARNESS_TODO §6). Baseline-first, regression budget:\n\n| # | Title |\n|---|---|\n| [#327](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/327) | 🟠 Batch the profiler's N+1 per-column top-values warehouse queries |\n| [#323](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/323) | 🟠 Index + batch the result-retention sweep for large `results` tables |\n| [#318](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/318) | Run progress is binary 0%→100% (GX atomic batch) — no true per-check incremental progress |\n\n---\n\n## Theme 8 — Datasource / connection depth\n\n| # | Title |\n|---|---|\n| [#194](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/194) | Snowflake key-pair: support encrypted (passphrase-protected) private keys |\n| [#195](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/195) | Snowflake key-pair: migrate GX runner off deprecated `kwargs['connect_args']` private_key path |\n| [#351](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/351) | Test Connection button on the New/Edit connection page (draft-connection test endpoint) |\n| [#244](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/244) | Suite-on-suite triggering (run a suite when another suite completes) |\n\n---\n\n## Theme 9 — Results & reporting depth\n\n| # | Title |\n|---|---|\n| [#345](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/345) | Results export — PDF report of an executed suite run |\n| [#283](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/283) | Check version history — restore/revert to a previous version |\n| [#349](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/349) | Results: dedupe the runs fetch across tabs + share the date-window presets with Dashboard |\n\n---\n\n## Theme 10 — Test-hardening & frontend-refactor backlog\n\nQuality debt deliberately parked until Week 8 / post-v1 (CONTRIBUTING rule 4a — periodic, not per-PR):\n\n| # | Title |\n|---|---|\n| [#278](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/278) | Triage the 63 mutmut survivors in `useAsyncAction` toast helper |\n| [#204](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/204) | Consolidate drawer/delete duplication (`confirmDelete`, `errorMessage`, submit guards) |\n| [#229](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/229) | Extract a shared `AsyncBody`/`AsyncTable` loading/error/empty helper |\n| [#236](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/236) | Extract a shared `connectionOptionLabel(c)` helper |\n| [#326](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/326) | `RunNowPanel`: redundant `{open && …}` guard alongside `Modal destroyOnClose` |\n| [#237](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/237) | `ImportSuiteDrawer`: unreachable empty-connections hint (dead UI) |\n\n---\n\n## Theme 11 — Design-only deferrals (no issue filed 59:### Deferred to post-v1 (design captured, not built in v1)
61:> **Canonical home: [docs/post-v1-roadmap.md](post-v1-roadmap.md)** — the single index of every deferred theme **and** the full `Backlog (post-v1 / testing)` issue list mapped by theme. The rows below are the headline design themes; the roadmap doc carries the rest (monitor kinds, alerting depth, consistency, perf/scale, connection depth, results/reporting, test-hardening backlog, design-only deferrals).
65:| **Admin / access model / UI & IA** — workspace-admin workspace-wide view (#411), admin write actions (#412), Settings/Profile IA, reusable notification channels. Intentionally **not** built in v1: single-tenant doesn't warrant an RBAC console — kept minimal, aim the foundations at the DQ loop instead. | [docs/post-v1-admin-ui-notes.md](post-v1-admin-ui-notes.md) + #411 / #412 (Backlog milestone) | 🔵 |
67:| **Expectation expansion, marketplace & LLM-assisted authoring** — the "intelligence" layer on the DQ loop: (1) the 5 high-ROI GX built-ins (`mostly` + compound/cross-column + type/set/date; aggregates likely a 3rd monitor kind, not a GX expectation), (2) LLM custom-SQL generator, (3) LLM curated check-suggestions, (4a) curated server-served expectation catalog (adds the server-side allowlist v1 lacks). LLM integration = an admin-configured, default-off, **BYO-credential `LLMProvider` seam** (Anthropic default · Azure Foundry · Bedrock · any OpenAI-compatible endpoint incl. local Ollama/vLLM/TGI), customer pays, no key-proxy, schema-only (PII-redacted) context. | [docs/post-v1-dq-intelligence-notes.md](post-v1-dq-intelligence-notes.md) | 🔵 |
152:> **Day 1 design decision: `check.kind` discriminator + numeric metric storage — ✅ settled in [ADR 0012](adr/0012-monitor-kind-seam.md); rides the same migration.** Keeps v1.x auto-monitors (freshness / volume / schema-drift / anomaly — post-v1 Theme A) from forcing a check/result schema rewrite. v1 implements `expectation` only.
197:- [x] ✅ Flat file check editor — file path + format selector — the datasource-shaped **run-target editor** on the suite drawer ([PR #246](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/246)) authors a flat-file target (`path` + optional CSV/Parquet `file_format`), so ADLS/S3 suites become runnable; the generic catalog editor ([PR #203](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/203)) already covers flat-file expectations. _(Container/regex **batch-resolution** inputs remain a post-v1 polish — needs the flat-file listing surface.)_
211:> Model the UI on [GX Cloud](https://greatexpectations.io/gx-cloud/): dedicated **full-page create flows** that **classify** what you're creating, keeping the lighter drawers for *edit*. Plan: `~/.claude/plans/fancy-moseying-raccoon.md`. Decided: in-app results page (Grafana deferred to an optional post-v1 ops add-on, never the per-suite authz path — ADR 0018 pending); full-page create + drawer edit; connections/checks redesign now (backends ready), results after the Week-5 run-enablement backend.
227:- [x] ✅ Generalise `run_suite` worker dispatch — select the `CheckRunner` by `connection.type` via the runner registry (`build_check_runner`), replacing the Snowflake-hardcoded wiring in `worker/tasks.py`; the seam that makes the flat-file / UC run paths route correctly and post-v1 RDBMS adapters a drop-in — [PR #146-fix](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/146) _(added per [ADR 0011](adr/0011-extensibility-seams-for-deferred-integrations.md); profiler dispatch collapsed onto a parallel registry in the same effort, [#147](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/147))_
264:> **Build as an in-app React page** (the GX-Cloud-style redesign Phase C), **not Grafana** — reading Postgres directly would bypass DataQ's per-suite sharing + PII redaction; Grafana is deferred to an optional post-v1 ops add-on ([ADR 0018](adr/0018-results-surface-and-grafana-deferral.md), accepted). The Week-5 run-enablement backend it gates on is **done**: `runs.py` read endpoints (`GET /runs`, `GET /runs/{id}`, `GET /pipeline_runs`) + `POST /suites/{id}/run` (PR-C0b); [#215](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/215) target (PR-C0a). The `results.metric_value`/`duration_ms` columns were seam-built for these trend charts (ADR 0012).
279:- [x] ✅ `ResultPublisher` seam — run outcomes dispatched from the worker's post-`execute_run` completion point through a `ResultPublisher` interface (Teams is the v1 impl, not a hardcoded call); PII redaction policy on `sample_failures` applied at the seam (`alerting/builder.redact` via `run_service.redact_sample_failures`). No-op publisher when unconfigured. Post-v1 JIRA/TestRail/Xray slot in as additive impls (ADR 0011). **[PR #366](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/366)**
287:> Adopt the **DataQ Design System** prototype (claude.ai/design project *"DataQ Design System"*, reverse-engineered from the shipped indigo/antd app — its `readme.md` is the spec) — **the full 13-screen set this week**, not a subset (ADR [0022](adr/0022-week6-prototype-adoption-and-chart-library.md); the earlier 6-task scope under-counted it — Profile content, **Settings** (pulled in from Week 7), the Admin layout-reconcile, and the New-Connection source-select split were missing). The **foundation already matches** the product (`theme.ts` palette, `resultsFormat.ts` status model, the 56px-header/220px-sider shell), so this is **not a re-skin** — it adopts the prototype's **structure**: every create/edit flow becomes a dedicated, deep-linkable **page** (the **Share** drawer stays the *only* drawer), login lands on an **Enhanced Monitoring Dashboard**, and standard **error pages** ship. **Where the prototype differs from the shipped app (drawer-vs-page, layout), the prototype wins.** Components are reproduced with **real antd** — the prototype's framework-free recreations + `_ds_bundle.js` are reference only (per the design README). Post-v1 items (marketing, dark mode) + the polish passes (KPI honesty, visual-fidelity) stay in Week 7 / Post-v1 below. The intentionally-dropped prototype bits (the **"View as" role switch**, the **component bundle**, the **teal palette**) are recorded in Post-v1 as *not adopted*.
360:- [ ] ⬜ **Dashboard KPI honesty pass** — the Dashboard renders only metrics with a real data model: pass-rate, run counts, and `metric_value`-backed trends (ADR 0012 seam). **Omit "anomalies detected"** (the `anomaly` monitor-kind is reserved, not implemented in v1) and **"avg. time to resolution"** (there is no incident/resolution model). Re-introduce when the monitor kinds / model exist (post-v1 Theme A). _Design ref: `templates/app/DashboardScreen.jsx` (the KPI card row — keep pass-rate / runs / trends, drop the two unbacked KPIs)._
361:- [ ] ⬜ **Visual-fidelity pass to the design spec** — self-hosted **Inter** + **JetBrains Mono** `@font-face` (if not already wired), motion tokens (120–180ms ease on bg/border/shadow, 0.5px press nudge), the 2px indigo focus ring, hover elevation on interactive cards, and empty/loading states — matched to the prototype's `guidelines/*` foundation cards. Keep using **semantic tokens** so post-v1 dark mode inherits for free. _Design ref: `guidelines/{type-body,type-mono,color-indigo,color-neutrals,color-status,spacing-scale,elevation,radius}.card.html` + `tokens/{fonts,typography,elevation,spacing,colors}.css` + `assets/fonts/`; readme §"Visual foundations" / "Motion" / "Focus" / "Iconography"._

1	# Post-v1 roadmap — the single home for deferred work
2	
3	> **What this is:** the canonical index of everything deliberately deferred past DataQ v1 —
4	> design themes (with their detailed design docs) **and** the concrete issue backlog. Every
5	> issue on the GitHub **`Backlog (post-v1 / testing)`** milestone is, by definition, post-v1
6	> and is mirrored here under the theme it belongs to.
7	>
8	> **What this is not:** a commitment or a schedule. v1 is the 8-week product (see
9	> [DataQ_platform_roadmap.md](DataQ_platform_roadmap.md) +
10	> [progress.md](../docs/progress.md)). These are the things we consciously chose *not* to build for v1,
11	> captured so the intent isn't lost.
12	>
13	> **Source of truth for status:** the GitHub milestone. This doc is the human-readable map;
14	> when an issue closes, it closes on GitHub — don't hand-maintain `[x]` here.
15	
16	**Detailed design docs** (the "why" + the shape):
17	- [post-v1-admin-ui-notes.md](../docs/post-v1-admin-ui-notes.md) — admin / access model / UI & IA
18	- [post-v1-dq-intelligence-notes.md](../docs/post-v1-dq-intelligence-notes.md) — expectation expansion, LLM-assisted authoring, marketplace
19	- [compliance-posture.md](../docs/compliance-posture.md) — GDPR / CCPA-CPRA / HIPAA technical controls + gap list
20	
21	**The big picture:** v1 ships the DQ loop (checks → results → trends → freshness/volume monitors
22	→ alerts → MCP) end-to-end. Post-v1 layers *intelligence* (more expectations, LLM authoring,
23	the remaining monitor kinds), *governance* (admin console, compliance controls), and *scale*
24	(performance hardening, second-impl seams) on top of it.
25	
26	---

60	
61	> **Canonical home: [docs/post-v1-roadmap.md](post-v1-roadmap.md)** — the single index of every deferred theme **and** the full `Backlog (post-v1 / testing)` issue list mapped by theme. The rows below are the headline design themes; the roadmap doc carries the rest (monitor kinds, alerting depth, consistency, perf/scale, connection depth, results/reporting, test-hardening backlog, design-only deferrals).
62	
63	| Theme | Where the design lives | Status |
64	|---|---|---|
65	| **Admin / access model / UI & IA** — workspace-admin workspace-wide view (#411), admin write actions (#412), Settings/Profile IA, reusable notification channels. Intentionally **not** built in v1: single-tenant doesn't warrant an RBAC console — kept minimal, aim the foundations at the DQ loop instead. | [docs/post-v1-admin-ui-notes.md](post-v1-admin-ui-notes.md) + #411 / #412 (Backlog milestone) | 🔵 |
66	| **Compliance posture (GDPR / CCPA-CPRA / HIPAA)** — technical-controls audit + gap list. v1 = privacy-by-design (PII redaction, retention purge, least-priv secrets, suite-scoped authz, BYOL controller/processor split). v2.x gaps to claim "processor-grade controls": access audit trail (#431, HIPAA gate), data-subject rights (#432), authoritative PII/PHI classification (#433), residency assertion (#434), CMK (#435). Not a legal certification — org-side artifacts (DPA/BAA/DPIA) stay the controller's. | [docs/compliance-posture.md](compliance-posture.md) + #431–#435 (Backlog) | 🔵 |
67	| **Expectation expansion, marketplace & LLM-assisted authoring** — the "intelligence" layer on the DQ loop: (1) the 5 high-ROI GX built-ins (`mostly` + compound/cross-column + type/set/date; aggregates likely a 3rd monitor kind, not a GX expectation), (2) LLM custom-SQL generator, (3) LLM curated check-suggestions, (4a) curated server-served expectation catalog (adds the server-side allowlist v1 lacks). LLM integration = an admin-configured, default-off, **BYO-credential `LLMProvider` seam** (Anthropic default · Azure Foundry · Bedrock · any OpenAI-compatible endpoint incl. local Ollama/vLLM/TGI), customer pays, no key-proxy, schema-only (PII-redacted) context. | [docs/post-v1-dq-intelligence-notes.md](post-v1-dq-intelligence-notes.md) | 🔵 |
68	
69	---

543	
544	## How to update this file
545	
546	When merging a PR:
547	
548	1. Find the task(s) it implements in the relevant week.
549	2. Flip `⬜` → `✅` (or `⬜` → `🟡` if partial).
550	3. Append the PR link: `— [PR #N](https://github.com/.../pull/N)`.
551	4. Update the per-week subtotal at the bottom of the week.
552	5. Update the **Snapshot** table at the top (task count, open PRs/issues) and the **Aggregate** table.
553	6. If the PR added an out-of-roadmap task (e.g. ADR-driven scope change), add a row with the note.
554	
555	PR-template checkbox enforces this. If the change is purely tooling / docs that doesn't map to a roadmap task, tick the "N/A" checkbox.
556	
```

278:## 13. Status & current milestone
282:**Current week:** **Week 7 of 8 — Deployment, hardening & docs — IN PROGRESS; DataQ v1 is now DEPLOYED TO AZURE and reachable** (Weeks 1–6 complete, all exit gates met). **Cloud deploy (2026-06-28):** the in-repo Terraform (`deploy/terraform/`, ADR 0024) stood up the app stack into `dataq-rg` — `dataq-app-{api,worker}` + `dataq-app-migrate` job (GHCR slim image, ADR 0025) on the **shared `dataq-cae`** Container Apps env, `dataq-app-redis` (password-auth), Key Vault (UAMI) + App Insights + Log Analytics, and **`dataq-app-web`** Static Web App with the api **linked as same-origin `/api` backend**. The app's DB is a distinct **`dataq`** database + least-priv **`dataq_app`** role on the **shared `dataq-pg-wus3-*`** server (1-of-each free/trial cap → env + Postgres shared with the harness, neutral-named `purpose=dataq-shared`; harness Postgres backed-up→recreated→restored). Azure AD **SSO app registrations** (API + SPA) created in TF + wired; migrate job ran `alembic upgrade head`; API healthy (401 = auth-enforced), SPA + deep-links 200, GitHub OIDC secrets/vars + `production` env set. Fixed **#393** (opencensus AzureLogHandler `lock=None` on Py3.13) en route. **GHCR package→repo connect done** (Actions-access grant → CI's `GITHUB_TOKEN` can push) and the **Deploy workflow validated end-to-end** (#403 fixed the migrate-command + frontend-pnpm bugs #401/#402; build→push→`alembic upgrade head`→ACA roll + SWA deploy all green on `v6`). **Post-deploy hardening (2026-06-28):** two production bugs surfaced and fixed — **#405** (Celery beat crashed on startup: the embedded `worker -B` beat re-nulled `self.lock` inside the opencensus `AzureLogHandler.createLock` fork on Python 3.13, silently killing ALL periodic tasks — orchestration polling, scheduled-suite dispatch, gap recovery, and sample-failure purge; fixed by making `createLock` idempotent in `backend/app/core/logging.py` + a network-free regression test; **#407** merged) and **#406** (deployed app couldn't read Key Vault: `AzureKeyVaultStore` called `DefaultAzureCredential()` with no args but the api+worker container runs a USER-assigned managed identity and `AZURE_CLIENT_ID` was unset — blocked connection tests, suite runs, AND orchestration polling; fixed by adding `AZURE_CLIENT_ID = azurerm_user_assigned_identity.app.client_id` to `local.app_env` in `deploy/terraform/containerapps.tf`; **#408** merged). Backend image `:v7` built+pushed from main (with #405+#406); api+worker rolled to v7; App Insights re-enabled on the worker (the #405 mitigation of temporarily dropping `APPLICATIONINSIGHTS_CONNECTION_STRING` on the worker is reverted) — tracked as **#409** (open; bumps `image_tag` default v4→v7 in `deploy/terraform/variables.tf`). **Orchestration polling is now live end-to-end** — beat starts clean (zero NoneType crashes) with App Insights on, api healthy (401, auth-enforced), ADF+Airflow connections polling via the 10-min beat fallback, Key Vault secrets read successfully. **Remaining W7:** Azure Monitor→ADF webhook alert rule, the FastMCP 8-tool server, and the hardening pass. Week-6 close: the **alerting backend track** — `ResultPublisher` seam (#366), Teams adaptive card + publisher (#367), severity-aware routing (#368), dedup (#369), suppression/snooze (#370), per-suite notification config (#373) — plus the **prototype Phase 5–6 screens**: Profile content (#374), Workspace Settings (#375), Admin layout-reconcile (#376), standard 4xx/5xx error pages (#377), and the per-suite **notification config UI** (#378). Earlier Week-6: Results scaffold (PR-C1), Enhanced Monitoring Dashboard + run-detail route (#333), results filter bar + orchestration poll/correlation (#347), drawer→page restructures (#350), layout/prototype-fidelity polish (#353), and redacted sample failing rows (#365, closed #226). **Week-7 early-credit:** Azure deploy scaffolding (#379 — frontend Dockerfile + ACA/SWA manifests + parameterized deploy workflow + CORS middleware + prod env reference; manual-trigger only, the actual apply stays blocked on Azure RP registration per ADR 0021). _All Week-6 feature work **merged to `main`** as the stacked PR chain #366→#379; follow-ups #380 (W6 close-out docs), #381 (deploy migration-gate + doc reconcile — a Week-7 CI/CD task landed early), and #390 (live run-progress per-status histogram fix, closes #316) merged after._
297:**Next milestone:** **Week 7 — Deployment, hardening & docs (in progress).** Exit gate: production-ready v1 deployed to Azure, CI/CD live, team onboarded. **Cloud deploy is DONE** (ADR 0024/0025 — app live on ACA + SWA). **Post-deploy hardening DONE** — Celery beat fix (#407, closes #405) + Key Vault credential fix (#408, closes #406) + image `:v7` redeploy (#409 open) — orchestration polling (ADF + Airflow), scheduled-suite dispatch, gap recovery, and periodic purge are all live. **Remaining:** Azure Monitor→ADF webhook alert rule, the **FastMCP server (8 tools)**, the security hardening pass (Postgres VNet-private + KV purge-protection toggles already in TF), and team onboarding docs.
298:**Active blockers:** none in-repo. Open follow-ups: **#409** (v7 image-tag TF bump, open); profiler N+1 batching [#327](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/327); validation-handler `ctx` serialization [#371](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/371) + `SecretStore.delete` secret cleanup [#372](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/372) (both filed in W6); Week-6 follow-ups [#348](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/348)/[#349](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/349)/[#351](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/351)/[#352](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/352); Week-4 nits [#197](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/197)/[#199](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/199)/[#204](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/204)/[#205](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/205); backend [#194](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/194)/[#195](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/195). See [docs/progress.md](docs/progress.md).
```

Week 7 — Deployment, hardening & docs (tracking epic)
---BODY---
Tracking epic for **Week 7 — Deployment, hardening & docs**. Mirrors the roadmap; the task-level source of truth remains [docs/progress.md](docs/progress.md). Milestone: `Week 7 — Deployment, hardening & docs`.


**DevOps & deployment**
- [ ] Containerise FastAPI + React + Celery + Redis
- [ ] Push images to Azure Container Registry
- [ ] Deploy to Azure Container Apps (API + Celery worker) + Azure Static Web App (React UI)
- [x] CI/CD pipeline — lint, test, build, deploy (Deploy workflow validated end-to-end + GHCR connect; #403 fixed #401/#402; push-on-merge still off)
- [ ] Application Insights integration — traces, errors, slow queries, Celery task metrics

**Azure Monitor webhook setup (one-time infra, post-deployment)**
- [ ] Create Action Group (pre-prod) — type: Webhook, URL: `https://<pre-prod-api>.azurecontainerapps.io/api/v1/adf/events`, shared secret: value from Key Vault
- [ ] Create Alert Rule (pre-prod) — scope: `example-adf-preprod` factory, signal: `Failed pipeline runs`, dimension: all pipelines (tick "include all future values"), action: Action Group above
- [ ] Create Action Group (prod) — same config pointing to prod API URL
- [ ] Create Alert Rule (prod) — scope: `example-adf-prod` factory, same signal + dimension config
- [ ] Smoke test — trigger a deliberate pipeline failure in DEV, confirm webhook fires, DB updates, and ADF panel reflects failure within 5 min

**FastMCP — MCP server**
- [ ] Install FastMCP, scaffold `mcp_server.py`, mount MCP app into FastAPI at `/mcp` — `app.mount("/mcp", mcp.get_asgi_app())`
- [ ] Wire Azure AD token validation into FastMCP's auth provider — validate Bearer token on every MCP tool call using the same `verify_azure_ad_token` logic as the REST API
- [ ] Implement `list_suites` resource — all suites accessible to current user, name / datasource / env / last run status / check count
- [ ] Implement `get_suite_results` resource — latest DQ run results for a suite; pass/fail per check, observed vs expected values, sample failing rows
- [ ] Implement `get_health_score` resource — overall health score and 7-day trend, filterable by env and datasource type
- [ ] Implement `get_adf_pipeline_status` resource — latest ADF pipeline run status with correlated DQ result per pipeline
- [ ] Implement `trigger_suite_run` tool — async GX suite execution against a given datasource + env; returns `run_id` for polling
- [ ] Implement `get_run_status` tool — poll live check-by-check progress for a running execution using `run_id`
- [ ] Implement `create_check` tool — add a new GX expectation to a suite (form path: check type + column + threshold; or raw SQL)
- [ ] Implement `profile_column` tool — run column profiler on a table or file; returns nulls, distinct count, min/max, top values
- [ ] Write LLM-optimised docstrings for all 8 tools — describe what the tool does, when to use it, what parameters mean, and what the response contains; tested against realistic natural language queries
- [ ] Test end-to-end with Claude Desktop — verify tool selection is correct for: "what failed today?", "run the orders suite on DEV", "why did the customer pipeline fail?", "add a null check on email"
- [ ] Document MCP connection config for Claude Desktop, Claude.ai, GitHub Copilot (`mcp.json`), and Cursor (`~/.cursor/mcp.json`) in README

**Hardening & docs**
- [ ] E2E test coverage for critical paths (auth, Snowflake run, flat file run, UC run, results)
- [ ] Error handling audit — all API endpoints return consistent error shapes
- [ ] Ensure all FastAPI endpoints have `summary`, `description`, `tags`, and `response_model` for clean Swagger output
- [ ] README + deployment guide + environment variable reference
- [ ] Team onboarding session + feedback collection


**Prototype adoption — final polish passes (added — not in original roadmap)**
> DataQ Design System prototype; closes out the prototype after the Week-6 structural pass (the full screen set — incl. **Settings** — now lands in Week 6, ADR [0022](../blob/main/docs/adr/0022-week6-prototype-adoption-and-chart-library.md)). What remains here is the KPI honesty + visual-fidelity passes. Mirrors the matching group in [docs/progress.md](docs/progress.md).
- [x] Backend: workspace-admin list-all endpoint (`GET /admin/suites` · `/users` · `/access`) — **shipped early in Week 4 (#289)**
- [x] Admin control centre page (`/admin`, `AdminScreen`) — real server-side 403 for non-admins (not a client "View as" toggle) — **shipped early in Week 4 (#289)**; Week 6 reconciles its layout to the prototype
- [ ] Dashboard KPI honesty pass — only real-data KPIs (omit "anomalies detected" / "avg. time to resolution")
- [ ] Visual-fidelity pass to the design spec — Inter / JetBrains Mono fonts, motion / focus tokens


**Live test/demo-data environment (v1-supporting harness — external, ADR [0021](../blob/main/docs/adr/0021-demo-test-data-environment-strategy.md))**
> Out-of-repo harness (Terraform / retail mock-data generators / Databricks notebook are **not** git-tracked); the mechanism that discharges the standing *"deferred live warehouse/file run smoke"*. Detailed steps live in the local `HARNESS_TODO.md`; mirrors the *Live test/demo-data environment* sub-section in [docs/progress.md](docs/progress.md).
- [ ] Terraform infra bootstrap — ADLS / Snowflake / Unity Catalog / ADF (Airflow runs **local via docker-compose**, not Terraform-provisioned in the v1 profile; datasources point at real cloud accounts)
- [ ] Retail data model + mock-data generators (Product / Inventory / Location / Sales / Returns / Logistics / Guest + discount/promo/tax/FX/price; **seed deliberate DQ failures** so checks actually fail)
- [ ] Reference **Flow A** — file lands in ADLS → ADF/Airflow loads to Snowflake/UC → DataQ checks the warehouse table
- [ ] Reference **Flow B** — file lands in UC RAW → Databricks notebook RAW→Silver→Gold → DataQ checks UC tables
- [ ] Reference **Flow C** — file lands in ADLS, stays flat → DataQ checks it in place (`FlatFileCheckRunner` + batch resolution)
- [ ] Discharge the deferred live warehouse/file run smoke once a flow runs green end-to-end (flip the ⬜ in `docs/progress.md` + note in CLAUDE.md §13)
```

**It should be 181, not 183.** The aggregate table is the only fully self-consistent artifact:
- **Rows:** 10+19+18+26+18+27+33+30 = **181**
- **Columns:** Done 114 + In-progress 15 + Pending 52 = **181**

**Why 181 is correct:** the aggregate table is the only internally self-consistent ledger —
- Row totals: 10+19+18+26+18+27+33+30 = **181**
- Column totals: Done 122 + In-progress 15 + Pending 44 = **181**

306	- [x] ✅ **Post-deploy bug: Celery beat crash on startup (closes [#405](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/405))** — the embedded `worker -B` beat process was re-nulling `self.lock` inside `AzureLogHandler.createLock()` on Python 3.13 (a recurrence of the #393 lock issue in the beat fork), silently killing **all** periodic tasks — orchestration polling (ADF + Airflow), scheduled-suite dispatch, gap recovery, and the sample-failure purge. Fix: override `createLock` in `backend/app/core/logging.py` to be idempotent (only ever create a missing lock, never null/swap one) + a network-free regression test in `backend/tests/core/test_logging.py`. — **[#407](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/407)**
307	- [x] ✅ **Post-deploy bug: deployed app couldn't read Key Vault (closes [#406](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/406))** — `AzureKeyVaultStore` called `DefaultAzureCredential()` with no args, but the api+worker containers run a USER-assigned managed identity and `AZURE_CLIENT_ID` was unset in the Container Apps env, so every `get_secret` call failed (`SecretNotFoundError` / `DefaultAzureCredential failed`). This blocked connection tests, suite runs, AND orchestration polling. Fix: add `AZURE_CLIENT_ID = azurerm_user_assigned_identity.app.client_id` to `local.app_env` in `deploy/terraform/containerapps.tf` (shared by the api and worker). — **[#408](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/408)**

342	> Follow-ups surfaced by the ACID / SCD review (2026-06-20). Single-DB ACID is sound (Postgres + unit-of-work + idempotent `ON CONFLICT` upserts); these close the deliberate gaps. Not in the original 100-task roadmap — tracked here so they aren't lost.
343	- [ ] ⬜ **Trigger-dedup race** — `_trigger_suites` is a non-atomic SELECT-then-INSERT with no unique constraint on `runs(suite_id, triggered_by)`; concurrent ingestion (webhook / poll / gap-recovery) can double-trigger a suite run. Fix = partial unique index + `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`. [#308](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/308)
344	- [ ] ⬜ **Stuck-run reaper** — a run committed `queued` before `send_task` is orphaned forever if the process dies in that window (no reaper; gap-recovery covers `pipeline_runs`, not `runs`). Beat janitor to fail/re-dispatch runs stuck past a threshold. [#309](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/309)
345	- [x] ✅ **History/audit strategy (ADR)** — decided in [ADR 0020](adr/0020-history-and-audit-strategy.md): per-entity **Type-4** snapshot tables where config history is needed (no SCD-2; credentials never snapshotted; cascade-delete accepted; soft-delete + cross-entity audit log deferred). **First implementation: `connection_versions`** (connection name/config history, snapshot on create + name/config update; secret-only rotation records nothing). [#310](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/310) _(audit-log + soft-delete remain deferred there)_
346	- [ ] ⬜ **Minor robustness (no separate issue)** — concurrent check-edit collision → 409 not an unhandled 500 (`IntegrityError` on the `uq_check_versions_check_version` backstop); explicit `rollback` in `get_db`'s `except`; document the "read-modify-write needs a unique constraint or `FOR UPDATE`" convention in CONTRIBUTING (no row-locking exists today, so every check-then-write is only as safe as its constraint).

## What was actually happening in Week 7 & 8

I recounted both weeks directly from the section checkboxes (status-aware: ✅ = done, `[x] 🟡` = in-progress/partial, ⬜/🔵 = pending), excluding only work the doc explicitly fences as out-of-roadmap.

**Week 7 — was `33`, actually `40`:**
The section had drifted because tasks were added inline without re-tallying. Its six groups are DevOps (9, incl. the 3 post-deploy bug-fix tasks) + Azure Monitor (5) + FastMCP (12) + Hardening/docs (6) + Consistency (4) + Prototype-polish (4) = **40**. Done = 10 ✅ (3 cloud-deploy + CI/CD + redeploy + 2 post-deploy + history-ADR + 2 admin). The 8 fenced "out-of-roadmap work landed" items (alerting publishers, monitors, etc.) are correctly *excluded*. The old `9/35` was both stale and undercounted.

**Week 8 — was `30`, actually `29`:**
Backend (12 — header wrongly said 11) + API (6, excluding the out-of-roadmap `Probe endpoints` test) + Frontend (8) + Infra (3) = **29**. Done = 1 ✅ (only AuthGate is fully complete; the 9 `[x] 🟡` lines are partial coverage → in-progress, which is why the old `~4` estimate was fuzzy).

{
  "command": "cd /Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ; python3 - <<'PY'\nimport re\nlines=open(\"docs/progress.md\").read().splitlines()\nheads=[]\nfor i,l in enumerate(lines):\n    m=re.match(r'^## Week (\\d) —',l)\n    if m: heads.append((int(m.group(1)),i))\n    if l.startswith('## Aggregate'): heads.append(('END',i))\nranges={}\nfor idx in range(len(heads)-1):\n    num,start=heads[idx]; end=heads[idx+1][1]\n    if num!='END': ranges[num]=(start,end)\n\ndef flags(l):\n    f=[]\n    if 'moved to Week' in l: f.append('MOVED-OUT')\n    if 'moved from Week' in l: f.append('MOVED-IN')\n    if 'out-of-roadmap' in l.lower(): f.append('OOR-TAG')\n    return f\n\nfor wk in sorted(ranges):\n    start,end=ranges[wk]\n    in_oo=False\n    cls_lines=[]\n    for i in range(start,end):\n        l=lines[i]\n        if l.startswith('### Out-of-roadmap'): in_oo=True\n        elif l.startswith('### '): in_oo=False\n        m=re.match(r'^- \\[([ x~-])\\]',l)\n        if not m: continue\n        status='✅' if '✅' in l else ('🟡' if '🟡' in l else ('🔵' if '🔵' in l else '⬜'))\n        fl=flags(l)\n        if in_oo: fl.append('OOR-SECTION')\n        cls_lines.append((status,fl,l[:80]))\n    # tally excluding OOR + MOVED-OUT\n    d=ip=pe=excl=0\n    for status,fl,_ in cls_lines:\n        if 'OOR-SECTION' in fl or 'OOR-TAG' in fl or 'MOVED-OUT' in fl:\n            excl+=1; continue\n        if status=='✅': d+=1\n        elif status=='🟡': ip+=1\n        else: pe+=1\n    print(f\"\\n===== Week {wk}: counted total={d+ip+pe} (done{d}/inprog{ip}/pend{pe}); excluded={excl} =====\")\n    for status,fl,txt in cls_lines:\n        tag=(' <<'+','.join(fl)+'>>') if fl else ''\n        print(f\"  {status} {txt}{tag}\")\nPY",
  "description": "Dump all checkboxes per week with classification + stray flags"
}

Tracking epic for **Week 8 — Unit testing & coverage (≥80%)**. Mirrors the roadmap; the task-level source of truth remains [docs/progress.md](docs/progress.md). Milestone: `Week 8 — Unit testing & coverage (≥80%)`.


**Backend unit tests (pytest)**
- [ ] Auth service — token validation, session expiry, Key Vault credential retrieval
- [ ] Connection service — CRUD operations, test endpoint logic per datasource type
- [ ] Suite service — CRUD, share assignment, export / import serialisation
- [ ] Check service — expectation builder (form path), SQL check validator, dry-run logic, threshold tier evaluation (warn/fail/critical status derivation from observed value + three thresholds, binary fallback when tiers not set)
- [ ] Column profiler service — null count, distinct count, min/max per datasource type
- [ ] Execution service — Celery task dispatch, progress polling, cancel logic, retention purge
- [ ] Alerting service — Teams webhook dispatch, dedup logic, snooze / suppression
- [ ] ADF service — webhook payload parsing (valid schema, missing fields, wrong secret → 401), follow-up REST API call for run details, upsert logic, gap recovery dedup
- [ ] ADF polling service — succeeded run fetch, skip-if-recently-updated logic, startup gap recovery
- [ ] Result service — health score calculation, historical trend aggregation, export generation
- [ ] MCP service — each of the 8 tools returns correct response shape; auth rejection on missing/invalid token; `trigger_suite_run` returns valid `run_id`; `create_check` persists to DB correctly

**API layer tests (pytest + httpx)**
- [ ] Auth endpoints — login redirect, token refresh, unauthorised access returns 401
- [ ] Connection endpoints — CRUD happy paths + validation errors
- [ ] Suite & check endpoints — CRUD, share, export / import, dry-run
- [ ] Execution endpoints — trigger run, poll progress, cancel, list history
- [ ] Results endpoints — dashboard data, drill-down, filters, download
- [ ] ADF webhook endpoint — valid payload returns 200, invalid secret returns 401, malformed payload returns 422, duplicate runId is idempotent

**Frontend unit tests (Vitest + React Testing Library)**
- [ ] Login screen — Azure AD button renders, redirects on click
- [ ] Connection manager — card renders per type, status badge colours, re-auth flow
- [ ] Check editor — form fields render per check type, column profiler loads, dry-run shows result, severity tier toggle shows / hides three threshold inputs correctly
- [ ] Suite sharing panel — add/remove user, role assignment
- [ ] Execution page — run now triggers correctly, progress bar updates, cancel button
- [ ] Results dashboard — stat cards render correct values, filters update table, ADF panel shows status, severity badges render correct colour for pass / warn / fail / critical

**Test infrastructure**
- [ ] Pytest fixtures — mock GX context, mock Snowflake / ADLS / UC connections, mock Celery, mock Azure Monitor webhook payload, mock ADF REST API run detail response
- [ ] CI gate — PRs blocked if unit test coverage drops below 80%
- [ ] Test data fixtures — sample suites, check results, run histories for consistent assertions


**QA/QE testing agent (added — not in original roadmap, ADR [0021](../blob/main/docs/adr/0021-demo-test-data-environment-strategy.md))**
> A Claude Code subagent (sibling to `migration-safety` / `orchestration-abstraction-guard`) that tests the DataQ framework module-by-module **and** E2E against the external demo-data harness. The one **in-repo** deliverable of the live test/demo-data environment workstream; detailed steps in the local `HARNESS_TODO.md`.

- [ ] Write `.claude/agents/qa-qe.md` (tools, model, system prompt)
- [ ] Wire it to drive the three reference flows (A/B/C) as E2E smoke once the harness is up
- [ ] Decide invocation cadence (per-PR / on-demand / pre-release)

- 4 files / +338 −53: `docs/progress.md` (W7 reconcile + recount to **188** + registers + post-v1 consolidation), `context/post-v1-roadmap.md` (new), `context/README.md`, `CLAUDE.md` §13.

Everything is now consistent end-to-end: the merged docs, the GitHub tracking epics (#175 → 28/28, #176 → 10/40, #177 → 1/29), and the `Backlog (post-v1 / testing)` milestone (#424 triaged). The gitignored local trackers (`WEEK7_TODO.md`, `HARNESS_TODO.md`) were correctly left out of the commit.

> AGENT

This is the classic full-history-scan problem. Two things jump out: `fetch-depth: 0` (fetches all history) + the `git` subcommand (walks **every commit** in history on every run). As the repo grows, that scan time grows unboundedly. Let me check the pre-commit setup and the config, then the docs.

=== .pre-commit-config betterleaks ===
45:  - repo: https://github.com/betterleaks/betterleaks

47-    hooks:
48:      - id: betterleaks
49-
50-  # ── General hygiene ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
51-  - repo: https://github.com/pre-commit/pre-commit-hooks
52-    rev: v5.0.0
53-    hooks:
54-      - id: trailing-whitespace
55-        # Preserve markdown's trailing-double-space line-break syntax in .md files
56-        args: [--markdown-linebreak-ext=md]
57-      - id: end-of-file-fixer
58-      - id: check-yaml
59-      - id: check-json
60-      - id: check-toml
--
62:      - id: detect-private-key         # belt-and-suspenders alongside betterleaks
63-      - id: no-commit-to-branch
64-        args: [--branch, main]         # prevents direct commits to main

=== .betterleaks.toml (head) ===
title = "DataQ betterleaks config"

# The global `filter` MUST be a top-level key, BEFORE [extend] — otherwise TOML
# scopes it under [extend] and betterleaks silently ignores it. A finding is
# skipped when this expression evaluates true (betterleaks discard-on-true).
#
# Skip ONLY Entire (entire.io) session-transcript checkpoints. Two path shapes:
#   - checkpoint shard commits:  <2hex>/<hex>/<seq>/full.jsonl  (e.g. c1/c6b5fdf0cb/0/full.jsonl)
#   - .entire metadata dir:      .entire/.../full.jsonl
# These are agent session logs that capture raw tool output (public download-URL
# hashes, sha256 digests, git SHAs) which trip high-entropy / 40-hex rules such as
# `sourcegraph-access-token` (e.g. the SWA CLI's StaticSitesClient@<40hex> URL).
# They live on an ephemeral checkpoint ref and never merge to main.
#
# Anchored to those exact shapes so a stray src/full.jsonl or evilfull.jsonl is
# still fully scanned. Verified (betterleaks v1.4.1, git mode): real secrets in
# normal files AND in src/full.jsonl / evilfull.jsonl are still reported; only the
# two checkpoint shapes are filtered; the default ruleset stays active (extend).
# Also verified the public checkpoint ref holds NO real credentials — only these
# hash false positives — so this filter hides false positives, not secrets.
filter = 'attributes[?"path"].orValue("").matches("^([0-9a-f]{2}/[0-9a-f]+/[0-9]+|\\.entire/.*)/full\\.jsonl$")'

[extend]
useDefault = true

# Betterleaks CI/Incremental Scanning

Based on the provided documentation, here's what's available for CI scanning:

## Git Scanning with Options

The basic git scan command is:
```
betterleaks git /path/to/repo -v --git-workers=16
```

## Directory Scanning Alternative

For scanning only changed files without full git history:
```
betterleaks dir /path/to/file/or/dir -v
```

## Notable Gap

The documentation shows "Scan Git" and "Scan local filesystem" as separate capabilities, but **does not explicitly document**:
- A `--log-opts` flag for commit range filtering (base..head)
- Official GitHub Actions integration
- Specific guidance on incremental PR scanning vs. full-history scanning

The page references a more detailed "[scanning doc](/betterleaks/betterleaks/blob/main/docs/scanning.md)" for "advanced scanning examples," suggesting that incremental/CI-specific instructions likely exist in that referenced document rather than on this landing page.

**Recommendation**: Check the `docs/scanning.md` file directly in the repository for CI/pull request-specific scanning strategies.
```

# Ways to Limit or Speed Up Scans

## Git Subcommand Flags

The `git` source offers several performance-tuning options:

- **`--git-workers`**: Run history scans in parallel. Example: `"betterleaks git . --git-workers 8"`
- **`--log-opts`**: Narrow the commit range with custom git log parameters. Example: `"betterleaks git . --log-opts='--all --since=90 days ago'"`

## Filesystem Scanning

- **`dir` without git history**: Use `"betterleaks dir ."` to scan only the current filesystem state, avoiding expensive history traversal
- **`--max-target-megabytes`**: Skip large files. Example: `"betterleaks dir . --max-target-megabytes 20"`

## Archive and Content Handling

- **`--max-archive-depth`**: Limit nested archive extraction. Example: `"betterleaks dir ./release-bundles --max-archive-depth 2"`
- **`--max-decode-depth`**: Control recursive content decoding to reduce overhead

## Object and Payload Size Limits

- **`--max-object-size`** (S3): Raise or lower per-object scanning limits (default: 250 MiB)
- **`--max-bucket-object-size`** (Hugging Face): Skip large bucket objects

## Concurrency Controls

- **`--workers`** (S3): Adjust concurrent GETs. Example: `"betterleaks s3 --workers=4 https://my-bucket.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/"`

## Pre-commit and Incremental Scanning

- **`--pre-commit`**: Scan only working tree diffs rather than full history
- **`--staged`**: Limit to staged changes: `"betterleaks git . --pre-commit --staged"`
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/.github/workflows/ci.yml",
  "old_string": "      # Betterleaks has no official GitHub Action; run the official pinned\n      # container image. Drop-in gitleaks replacement: nonzero exit on findings\n      # fails the job. safe.directory env is required because the container runs\n      # as root while checkout writes files as the runner user — without it git\n      # aborts with \"detected dubious ownership\".\n      - name: Run betterleaks\n        run: |\n          docker run --rm \\\n            -e GIT_CONFIG_COUNT=1 \\\n            -e GIT_CONFIG_KEY_0=safe.directory \\\n            -e GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_0=/repo \\\n            -v \"$PWD:/repo\" -w /repo \\\n            ghcr.io/betterleaks/betterleaks:v1.4.1 \\\n            git /repo --config /repo/.betterleaks.toml --redact --verbose",
  "new_string": "      # Scan only the commits THIS event introduces, not the whole history.\n      # A full `git` history walk grows unbounded with the repo (300+ commits →\n      # >15 min) and re-scans commits that were already scanned when they merged.\n      # Every line that reaches main is still scanned exactly once: a PR scans its\n      # own commits (base..head), and the push to main scans the squashed/merged\n      # commit (before..after) — the same model gitleaks-action uses.\n      - name: Compute commit range to scan\n        id: range\n        run: |\n          if [ \"${{ github.event_name }}\" = \"pull_request\" ]; then\n            echo \"logopts=--no-merges ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha }}..${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          elif [ -z \"${{ github.event.before }}\" ] || [ \"${{ github.event.before }}\" = \"0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\" ]; then\n            # new branch / no parent on record — scan just the tip commit\n            echo \"logopts=-1\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          else\n            echo \"logopts=--no-merges ${{ github.event.before }}..${{ github.sha }}\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          fi\n\n      # Betterleaks has no official GitHub Action; run the official pinned\n      # container image. Drop-in gitleaks replacement: nonzero exit on findings\n      # fails the job. safe.directory env is required because the container runs\n      # as root while checkout writes files as the runner user — without it git\n      # aborts with \"detected dubious ownership\". --log-opts limits the history\n      # walk to the computed range (passed via env to keep it a single arg).\n      - name: Run betterleaks (incremental — only this event's new commits)\n        env:\n          LOGOPTS: ${{ steps.range.outputs.logopts }}\n        run: |\n          docker run --rm \\\n            -e GIT_CONFIG_COUNT=1 \\\n            -e GIT_CONFIG_KEY_0=safe.directory \\\n            -e GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_0=/repo \\\n            -v \"$PWD:/repo\" -w /repo \\\n            ghcr.io/betterleaks/betterleaks:v1.4.1 \\\n            git /repo --config /repo/.betterleaks.toml --redact --verbose \\\n            --git-workers=4 \\\n            --log-opts=\"$LOGOPTS\""
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/.github/workflows/ci.yml",
  "old_string": "      - name: Compute commit range to scan\n        id: range\n        run: |\n          if [ \"${{ github.event_name }}\" = \"pull_request\" ]; then\n            echo \"logopts=--no-merges ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha }}..${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          elif [ -z \"${{ github.event.before }}\" ] || [ \"${{ github.event.before }}\" = \"0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\" ]; then\n            # new branch / no parent on record — scan just the tip commit\n            echo \"logopts=-1\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          else\n            echo \"logopts=--no-merges ${{ github.event.before }}..${{ github.sha }}\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          fi",
  "new_string": "      # All inputs here are GitHub-generated commit SHAs (no shell metacharacters),\n      # but pass them via env per workflow-injection best practice.\n      - name: Compute commit range to scan\n        id: range\n        env:\n          EVENT: ${{ github.event_name }}\n          PR_BASE: ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha }}\n          PR_HEAD: ${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}\n          PUSH_BEFORE: ${{ github.event.before }}\n          PUSH_AFTER: ${{ github.sha }}\n        run: |\n          if [ \"$EVENT\" = \"pull_request\" ]; then\n            echo \"logopts=--no-merges ${PR_BASE}..${PR_HEAD}\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          elif [ -z \"$PUSH_BEFORE\" ] || [ \"$PUSH_BEFORE\" = \"0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\" ]; then\n            # new branch / no parent on record — scan just the tip commit\n            echo \"logopts=-1\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          else\n            echo \"logopts=--no-merges ${PUSH_BEFORE}..${PUSH_AFTER}\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          fi"
}
```
