id: toolu_0193ZYB99EdhX73sU1W15V6N
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/.github/workflows/ci.yml",
  "old_string": "      # Betterleaks has no official GitHub Action; run the official pinned\n      # container image. Drop-in gitleaks replacement: nonzero exit on findings\n      # fails the job. safe.directory env is required because the container runs\n      # as root while checkout writes files as the runner user — without it git\n      # aborts with \"detected dubious ownership\".\n      - name: Run betterleaks\n        run: |\n          docker run --rm \\\n            -e GIT_CONFIG_COUNT=1 \\\n            -e GIT_CONFIG_KEY_0=safe.directory \\\n            -e GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_0=/repo \\\n            -v \"$PWD:/repo\" -w /repo \\\n            ghcr.io/betterleaks/betterleaks:v1.4.1 \\\n            git /repo --config /repo/.betterleaks.toml --redact --verbose",
  "new_string": "      # Scan only the commits THIS event introduces, not the whole history.\n      # A full `git` history walk grows unbounded with the repo (300+ commits →\n      # >15 min in CI) and re-scans commits already scanned when they merged.\n      # Every line that reaches main is still scanned exactly once: a PR scans its\n      # own commits (base..head), and the push to main scans the squashed/merged\n      # commit (before..after) — the model gitleaks-action uses. Verified locally:\n      # full history ~88s vs ranged ~0.5s, and a planted non-allowlisted secret in\n      # the range is still detected (exit 1).\n      #\n      # All inputs here are GitHub-generated commit SHAs (no shell metacharacters),\n      # but pass them via env per workflow-injection best practice.\n      - name: Compute commit range to scan\n        id: range\n        env:\n          EVENT: ${{ github.event_name }}\n          PR_BASE: ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha }}\n          PR_HEAD: ${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}\n          PUSH_BEFORE: ${{ github.event.before }}\n          PUSH_AFTER: ${{ github.sha }}\n        run: |\n          if [ \"$EVENT\" = \"pull_request\" ]; then\n            echo \"logopts=--no-merges ${PR_BASE}..${PR_HEAD}\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          elif [ -z \"$PUSH_BEFORE\" ] || [ \"$PUSH_BEFORE\" = \"0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\" ]; then\n            # new branch / no parent on record — scan just the tip commit\n            echo \"logopts=-1\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          else\n            echo \"logopts=--no-merges ${PUSH_BEFORE}..${PUSH_AFTER}\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          fi\n\n      # Betterleaks has no official GitHub Action; run the official pinned\n      # container image. Drop-in gitleaks replacement: nonzero exit on findings\n      # fails the job. safe.directory env is required because the container runs\n      # as root while checkout writes files as the runner user — without it git\n      # aborts with \"detected dubious ownership\". --log-opts limits the history\n      # walk to the computed range (passed via env to keep it a single arg);\n      # --git-workers parallelises the walk for larger ranges.\n      - name: Run betterleaks (incremental — only this event's new commits)\n        env:\n          LOGOPTS: ${{ steps.range.outputs.logopts }}\n        run: |\n          docker run --rm \\\n            -e GIT_CONFIG_COUNT=1 \\\n            -e GIT_CONFIG_KEY_0=safe.directory \\\n            -e GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_0=/repo \\\n            -v \"$PWD:/repo\" -w /repo \\\n            ghcr.io/betterleaks/betterleaks:v1.4.1 \\\n            git /repo --config /repo/.betterleaks.toml --redact --verbose \\\n            --git-workers=4 \\\n            --log-opts=\"$LOGOPTS\""
}
```

That grows unbounded with the repo (301\ncommits -> ~88s locally, >15 min on the runner) and re-scans commits already\nscanned when they merged.\n\nScan only the commits the event introduces, via `--log-opts`:\n- pull_request: `--no-merges <base.sha>..<head.sha>` (the PR's commits)\n- push:         `--no-merges <before>..<after>` (the squashed/merged commit),\n  with a `-1` tip-only fallback when there's no parent on record (new branch).\n\nEvery line that reaches `main` is still scanned exactly once (PR commits on the\nPR run + the squash commit on the push to main) — the same model\ngitleaks-action uses. Also adds `--git-workers=4` to parallelise larger ranges.\n\nVerified locally against the pinned v1.4.1 image: full history ~88s vs ranged\n~0.5s; empty range and `-1` fallback exit 0; a planted non-allowlisted secret\nin the range is still detected and exits 1 (gate still fails on findings).\nEvent SHAs are passed via env (workflow-injection best practice).\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\necho \"--- committed; pushing ---\"; git push -u origin chore/betterleaks-incremental-scan 2>&1 | tail -2",
